## 2026-07-30 (Opus 5, autonomous) — T4-CY COST REMOVED AND RE-ENABLED · T6-G GATE CLOSES THE RECONCILIATION CHAIN · TWO TICKETS SHIPPED WHOSE DEFECT WAS WIRING, NOT LOGIC — START HERE

**Session shape: five commits, four tickets moved, and three of them turned out to be about something other than what they said.**

- **T4-CY RE-ENABLED (`8ceaf73`).** `DeterministicProbeSynthesizer` builds probe sequences from the artifact's own sources with **zero model calls** — `400` → asserted request with an empty body · `404` → an id nothing created · `409` → a derived body sent twice. Measured over the live seed spec: **8 of 11 stated requirements executable**, where all eleven previously cost a synthesis call that could block for minutes. Model path survives as `synthesizeWithModel` behind a default-OFF flag.
  - **🔴 THE FIRST CUT WAS 9 of 11 AND TWO OF THE NINE WERE FALSE-CONTRADICTION GENERATORS.** Reading the probes instead of counting them: **`422` de-scoped** (its only instance here is "at most 3 active loans" — an empty body is refused as invalid input long before any limit, and reporting that 400 as a missing 422 is the harm class that got T2-EU and T2-EV reverted) · **a refused SETUP request is now an abstain** (the duplicate-loan probe's first POST is refused when the book/member it references do not exist; without the guard the second POST's 404 reads as "409 expected, got 404") · **the server-assigned `id` is dropped from creation bodies**. A test also caught a real defect first: `ground()` used `String.replaceFirst`, so `$1.id` was read as a regex group reference and threw.
- **T4-CY OBSERVABILITY, and it paid for itself in ten minutes (`4b810d5`).** Every reason for not observing was silent or at `debug`, so "never looked" was indistinguishable from "looked and found nothing" — the same blindness that let `criterion_probe_runs=0` stand for 14 runs. Now each abstention logs its reason at INFO. **The very first field run then said `the last compile probe was COMPILE_ERROR`, which is a diagnosis I could not get at all before.** Also fixed: the interval reset BEFORE the cheap gates, so a free skip burned a whole interval (run `21-39-59` got 4 looks in 41 cycles, was mid-repair at every one, and observed nothing); it now resets only where a package build and boot are actually spent.
- **T6-G MEASURED — VERDICT THIN, and the reconciliation chain closes on evidence (`3015196`).** `scripts/lineage_overlap.py` + 16 tests. The fixture was **26 lineages, not the 2 the ticket assumed**. 91.2% of criteria and 93.4% of goal descriptions are unique to ONE lineage; `codeDigest` — the identity axis the chain was to be built on — is populated on **2.4%** of criterion results; ecosystem facts are not per-lineage at all (one shared on-disk cache); **59 of the 87 shared criteria (67.8%) carry OPPOSITE verdicts**.
  - **The number that decided it came from refusing the aggregate.** The script's first verdict was MARGINAL at 6.6%; reading the 111 "transferable" items showed every one was the seed's root goal, its opening decomposition, or a template repair. Splitting overlap by the role that PRODUCED each goal turns that reading into a measurement: `system` 100% · `coverage-retro` 18.6% · `planner` 10.0% · **`analysis` — the loop's own discoveries — 3 of 288 = 1.0%** · `divided-criterion`/`verification-residue` 0%. On the original two-lineage fixture: **0 of 25**. Recorded on T4-CN/T4-CM/T4-CS with re-entry = a DIFFERENT-seed fixture or a semantic pass. **T6-H keeps independent value and is NOT closed.**
- **T3-BX (`d43f286`) — the rule already existed; its output never reached the agent that could act on it.** `WiringFactsAnalyzer`'s validation-surface check has been emitting exactly this fact for months. Over the real output trees of **six** corpora it fires on **all six** (incl. *"requests validation of Loan but Loan declares no field constraint annotations"* — why `POST /loans` answered 500 where the spec asks 400), while `peerAnnotationAnomalies()` — **the ONLY analyzer path into the evolution prompt** — returns **0 on all six**. `analyze()` goes to the criteria verifier and nowhere else. Shipped `statusRiskFacts()` (a status-relevant SUBSET, not the whole payload) into the evolution prompt, plus peer naming in the existing single home. **I first built a second vocabulary-free detector (132 lines), it worked, and I deleted it — one invariant, one home.**
- **✅ T2-CI CLOSED ON A FIELD FIRING, first run it was live (`2026-07-30_00-48-16`, 01:13:29) — verdict RIGHT, reasoning 3/5 NOISE, precision fixed the same hour.** The subgoal was "Create BookService.java", so five annotation losses across three files was suspicious enough to CHECK rather than celebrate. **True positive:** `BookRepository` lost `@Repository` with member names unchanged while `BookService` still constructor-injected it — unregistered bean, context fails to start, **file compiles perfectly**, i.e. exactly what the 2026-05-22 subsumption-by-compile-probe closure claimed was impossible. My heuristic was the ONLY dissenting voice (LLM said ACCEPTED 0.85; `DeterministicScoreHeuristic` said ACCEPTED) and its CRITICAL defect finding carried the rollback. **False component:** the same mutation dropped `@Data`/`@NoArgsConstructor`/`@AllArgsConstructor` from `Book` **and hand-wrote the eight members they generated** — a real substitution, wrongly flagged, with rationale telling evolution to keep the annotation. **Fix:** substitution is now recognised at the MEMBER level (a lost annotation is excused when the type declares member names it did not before; compared by NAME so a changed signature cannot launder a loss). Replaying the real snapshot against the real mutation drops the report **5 losses → 1**, and a faithful `@Service`-with-unchanged-members reconstruction still REJECTS. **Recall traded away and pinned by its own test:** add-a-member-plus-drop-a-stereotype is no longer reported (bounded by the boot gate and the peer rule). It broke one of my own earlier tests whose fixture happened to add a method while stripping the annotation. **Diagnosed against the per-cycle SNAPSHOT — the live tree changed between two of my own commands, so [[feedback-verify-at-decision-timestamp]] caught in the act.**
- **T2-CI as built (`14235c4`) needed none of the store, the marker list, or the goal-text override the ticket proposed.** `previousOutput()` already IS the record of what earlier merges established. `MergeRegressionHeuristic` rejects a candidate whose type loses annotations and adds none (strict loss), which also dissolves the migration override: a package migration rewrites imports and leaves simple names alone. **Verified on real artifacts** — stripping the stereotype from four corpora's actual controllers reproduces the field defect REJECTED 4/4, unchanged controls abstain 4/4; both sides of every pair compile, which is why the 2026-05-22 subsumption-by-compile-probe closure was wrong.
- **🔧 METHODOLOGY UNLOCK, measured: tests and live runs are NOT mutually exclusive.** `logback-spring.xml` takes its destination from `FILE_BASE_PATH` alone, so `FILE_BASE_PATH=<scratch> mvn test` runs the **full 2279-test suite during a live run with the live log byte-identical** (429257 → 429257, then growing normally). This removed the serialisation that used to make offline work wait out a 90-minute run. Folded into [[project-tests-rotate-live-logs]] as its third variant.
- **HARNESS FIX (`46923d1`):** preflight's G1 guard scanned every `/proc` cmdline for the jar name without checking the process was a JVM, so my own run monitor — which grepped for that name — was counted as five live JVMs and blocked a relaunch. Third time this self-match family has bitten. Guard now requires `comm == java`; `scripts/autobuilder_up.sh` holds the pattern so no caller's command line carries it.
- **MY RECURRING ERROR APPEARED TWICE MORE, both caught:** ① I read a `NOT_EVALUATED` on four real artifacts as the new heuristic failing on real input — the **probe** was passing a null `EvaluationOutcome`, which every heuristic here treats as no-opinion. The code was right; the instrument was not. ② I spent several steps hunting a phantom interval defect from a cycle count of 9 that was actually **5** (my `awk /CYCLE /` matched other lines; `behaviour.ndjson` `cycle_start` is the honest count).
- **RUN `2026-07-30_00-48-16` (deadline, 47 cycles, 12 post-merge builds, `smoke=artifact-build-failed`) — T2-CI's field firing came from here, and the run's FAILURE is a different and bigger finding.**
  - **My heuristic was 6 of the run's 8 rejections — checked for harm, and it HELD.** `BookRepository loses @Repository` appeared in 5 of 6 firings, which looked like blocking-without-convergence. It was not: the final artifact keeps `@Repository`, `@Service` AND the Lombok trio, so the rejections protected the invariants and the loop settled on the annotated versions. One firing of six was pure Lombok-substitution noise (fixed the same night by the member-level rule).
  - **🔴 THE RUN FAILED ON `Unable to find main class` — 5 files, one package, NO ENTRY POINT — and `T4-CV` never fired.** Its requirement sits at `GoalPlannerService:2815` on the ROOT CLOSURE path; the backlog never emptied, so the forward-looking entry-point requirement produced zero lines. **Per the ledger that is now 6 of 6 `artifact-build-failed` runs on the same message.** This is the same structural defect T4-CY was built to escape, still applying to the entry point.
  - **The diagnosis was in hand and discarded SEVEN times:** `BehaviouralSelfCheck` calls `packageGate.verifyPackageable()`, gets the blocker text, and drops it (`if (…isPresent()) return abstain(…)`). Only visible because the abstention logging shipped hours earlier. **Filed as `T4-CZ`, deliberately NOT BUILT** — the obvious fix (raise packaging work mid-run) is what T2-EQ did and got reverted after the user's explicit "packaging is an end-stage property" steer. The narrow survivable scope (entry point only, routed into T4-CV's existing injection) is written on the ticket and needs the user's call.
- **FIELD RUNS:** `2026-07-30_00-05-17` stopped by hand at 12 cycles to ship the observability fix. `2026-07-30_00-21-29` (90-min ceiling) is one of the **unproductive ~40%** — 23 min in at **2 Java files**, compile probe broken continuously, 8 self-check abstains all `COMPILE_ERROR`. **Verifiably not caused by my changes:** the jar predates T3-BX/T2-CI, deterministic synthesis only runs behind a package gate never reached, and the failures are in the model's own `LoanValidator`/`MemberValidator`. **T4-CY's DoD (a defect raised and repaired at normal throughput) is STILL UNMET — it needs a run whose build gets healthy.**
- **State:** suite **2292/0/5** · **14 commits this session, ALL unpushed (`origin/main..HEAD` = 9, so everything before this session had been pushed)** · T4-CY ON by default · T2-CI CLOSED on its field firing; T3-BX `[~]` awaiting one · 26 lineages in the graph · disk **91%**.
- **NEXT, in order:** ① a run that reaches a healthy build, to close T4-CY's field half — then **3+ runs at a FROZEN build**, which has still never been attempted ② watch for `T2-CI: rejecting a mutation that reverts established annotations` and for a `[10]`-class status check flipping (T3-BX field half) ③ `T6-H` (subtree outcome attribution) is the surviving live item of the wave-2 yardstick layer ④ do not chase process metrics for convergence; none predicts outcome.

## 2026-07-29 (Opus 5, continuation — ~36h session) — SELF-CORRECTION PROVEN THEN DISABLED ON COST; "REGRESSION" WAS A MISFRAMING — START HERE

**The loop observed its own running behaviour for the first time, and I had to switch it off.**
- **T4-CY WORKED (run `2026-07-28_23-16-15`).** Two mid-run observations; the criterion probes **executed for the first time in 14 runs** (`probed=6 contradicted=5`, then `6/3`); the defects were REAL (`POST /books`→500 vs spec 400, `GET /members/{}`→500 vs 404) and became work items. **The loop CAN see its own behaviour while it still has budget to fix it.**
- **🔴 DISABLED ON COST (`bc7aef1`), and my cost model was wrong TWICE.** Observations cost **29.5 + 14.9 min** at 73/77 requirements, then **19.7 + 14.9 min** at only 11. Neither list-length fix touched the cause: the time is in probe-**SYNTHESIS** calls that **TIME OUT** — `LLMClient` escalates its read timeout across retries and the check makes model calls **synchronously inside the cycle, competing with the loop's own use of the same local model**. Throughput 0.16–0.29 cyc/min vs ~0.65 normal; circuit breaker tripped twice. **RE-ENABLE only with DETERMINISTIC synthesis** — `RequestBodySynthesizer` + `{}` + an unused id + a repeated body cover the common cases in milliseconds.
- **T2-EW CORRECTED — I guarded one door of three.** Run `2026-07-29_00-54-22` failed on broken `BookControllerTest`/`BookServiceTest` while the log shows `MergeAgent` had **SKIPPED exactly those files**. `MutationApplier` writes evolution's mutations straight into `output/`; `CompileProbeService` stages there too. Now one home: `UnrequestedTestSourcePolicy`, consulted by both writers.
- **🔴 THE "REGRESSION" WAS A MISFRAMING OF MINE.** User asked why Jul 28→29 runs regressed. Answer: **they didn't, systematically.** July 28 in order: `build-failed, build-failed, build-failed, 23/23, 11/23, 10/23, 20/23, 20/23` — the day OPENED with three build failures. **23/23 was a PEAK, not a plateau.** Session scored runs: `21,19,21,22,23,11,10,20,20,14` → **mean 18.1, best 23, ≥22 in 2/10**. I called the 23/23 "the third 23/23" and treated it as a baseline; it was one sample, and I measured everything after against a high-water mark.
- **PROVEN NOT the cause** (each checked, not assumed): the 11/23 and 10/23 predate T4-CY and the only production change between them and the 23/23 (`c374758`) sits in a `GATE_BOOT` branch that logged **0 executions** in both. Model is healthy (22 GB on GPU, realistic 8 KB prompt → **3.46 s**, deterministic at temp 0 — already 0, hardcoded at `LLMClient:92`). Prompt size per call **flat** all session. Graph growth irrelevant (558 `Goal` nodes of 86k).
- **THE ONE DISCRIMINATOR THAT HOLDS: ~40% of runs never empty their backlog**, and that separates every good run from every bad one. Reaching the package gate is **necessary but not sufficient** (5 of 10 that reached it still failed). No process metric predicts a good run at n=15 — plan inflation (12 vs 23 injections) overlaps, stagnation rate shows no separation.
- **MY RECURRING ERROR, four times today:** verified a mechanism **FIRED** and inferred the **outcome followed** (dedup collapse, DI-check suspicion, T2-EW trap, T2-EW multi-door). [[feedback-activity-not-progress]] already names this; checking the artifact costs seconds.
- **Also:** smoke grades **10 of 23 checks on statuses the spec never states** (all 4 written codes are errors: 400/404/409/422; no success codes) — the loop passes all 10 convention checks and fails only stated ones, so the suite is LENIENT, not strict. `T4-CX` shipped (startup forecast moved behind the boot gate). `T5-BJ` filed (no index on `Goal`; latent, 558 nodes today). Live ceiling now **90 min**. Disk **91% full**.
- **State:** suite **2228/0/5** · **25 commits, 22 UNPUSHED** · T4-CY off by default · tree clean but `firestarter/cache/symbol-imports.json` (run-dirtied, deliberate).
- **NEXT, in order:** ① deterministic probe synthesis → re-enable T4-CY ② then **3+ runs at a FROZEN build** — the streak has never actually been tested, because code changed before nearly every run ③ do not chase process metrics for convergence; none predicts outcome.

## 2026-07-28 (Opus 5, long autonomous session) — THIRD 23/23 REACHED AND VERIFIED, BUT THE LOOP COULD NOT TELL — START HERE

**Headline: `2026-07-28_10-46-51` scored a hand-verified SMOKE 23/23** (rebuilt, booted, all 23 checks by hand) — third ever, after 07-07 and 07-24. **It ended `terminal=deadline`, not GOAL_FULFILLED.** It built a perfect artifact and had no idea.

- **🔴 COMPLETION IS DECIDED BY SOMETHING UNCORRELATED WITH CORRECTNESS.** Terminal history: `14-42-19 FULFILLED 21/23` · `16-41-48 FULFILLED 19/23` · `20-33-22 deadline 21/23` · `22-48-26 deadline 22/23` · `10-46-51 deadline 23/23`. **The last run to self-complete scored 19/23; the three best results are all timeouts.** Declaring fulfilled stops the work. A high score currently correlates with NOT completing.
- **Root cause: `criterion_probe_runs=0` in ALL 10 runs.** T4-CU (the only correctness signal) has never executed in the field. In `10-46-51`, `boot_gate_runs=3` — all three came through `EvaluationAgent`'s originating-gate re-run, which called the two-arg form with an **empty criteria list**. Fixed `c374758`; still unverified (the next run had `boot_gate_runs=0`).
- **SHIPPED 2026-07-27/28:** `T4-CU` + slice 2 (path-variable probing via `$N.field` response capture; reach over the smoke suite 9/23→13/23) · `T4-CV` (forward entry-point requirement — **field-verified, fires ~min 5, zero false positives across 4 runs**) · `T2-EU` (retired goal no longer blocks its replacement — **verified**) · `T2-EV` (spec-stated statuses as probeable requirements) · `T2-EW` (unrequested test sources not merged) · `T4-CX` (startup-wiring forecast moved behind the boot gate) · `T5-BJ` filed (no index on `Goal`).
- **🔴 TWO REGRESSIONS I SHIPPED AND HAD TO UNDO — both predicted by the user.** ① T2-EU first excluded EVERY SUBSUMED description: 8 of 18 active goals came back ≥0.88 similar to a retired one, two at 1.0000; backlog stopped emptying. Now scoped to the same planner response. ② T2-EV first attached spec statuses to the coverage work ITEM: the `POST /loans` item carried **12 criteria** while its mapping already existed, so it could never close — closure attempted once in 76 min, gates 0. Now supplied straight to the probe list. **Lesson: a requirement must be PROBEABLE, not OWNED by a work item.**
- **THE BOOT GATE IS NOT TOO STRICT — user proposed loosening it, evidence said no.** Its 3 blocks in the 23/23 run were a real `UnsatisfiedDependencyException` (context refresh cancelled, exit 1) and they are what drove the repairs that reached 23/23. Loosening it readmits false completion #3.
- **NO PROCESS METRIC PREDICTS A GOOD RUN (15-run corpus, measured).** Reaching the package gate is **necessary but not sufficient** (5 of 10 that reached it still failed; `01-01-01` reached it 15× and failed). Plan inflation: injections 12.2 reached vs 22.6 missed — direction consistent, ranges overlap. Stagnation-rate/throttle-bypass: **no separation** (23/23 run 0.18, failures 0.14–0.34). 3 of 15 runs scored ≥22 and nothing measurable says which.
- **VARIANCE DOMINATES:** same build, same 90-min ceiling → **23/23 then 11/23**. Single runs cannot adjudicate mechanism changes. I changed code before nearly every run all session, which is exactly what [[feedback-replay-over-batches]] forbids; the streak has never been attempted at a frozen build.
- **CORPUS REPLAY WORKS AND IS THE RIGHT INSTRUMENT.** Offline against 9-15 run_dirs, in minutes: T2-EW converts 2 of 4 build failures to packaging · all 8 observed smoke failures attributed 4/4 to T2-EV vs T4-CU · T4-CU's reach collapses with plan granularity (run `16-41-48` 19 of 27 criteria parseable → 22/23 checks reachable; `14-42-19` **7 of 132** → 7/23).

**User challenge that started it: "the runs all connect to one big graph instead of letting the previous ones being historical."** Their proposed cause (roots merged by matching description string) was checked and is NOT what happens — goals are `MERGE {id: goal-<uuid>}` and nothing keys on text. Dead `GoalGraphService` dep removed from `SystemResetConfig`.
- **SEQUENCING WAS LOAD-BEARING, and it was paid before flipping the switch.** Keeping history makes previously-safe reads dangerous. `findAllDescriptions` + `findDescriptionsByStatus` feed goal dedup and the GIVEN_UP unachievable guard → both `lineageId`-scoped, else a dead lineage's abandoned goals block a fresh one's subgoals.

Second boot: reset ran, **prior lineage's 3 nodes SURVIVED** (1383 new-lineage nodes beside them); goal dedup saw 8 goals / **0** prior (unscoped: 9 / **1** leaking); GIVEN_UP guard saw **0** (unscoped: **1**).

## 2026-07-24 late-4 (Opus 4.8) — FIELD VERIFICATION of T4-AP + T2-EP: caught & fixed a surface-1 regression
**User asked to verify T4-AP + T2-EP via a live run + smoke. Verification did its job — caught a real regression I shipped in T2-EP surface 1.**
- **Run 1** (`/tmp/preflight_runs/2026-07-24_19-52-56`, terminal=deadline, 54 cycles): T4-AP verified (0 context-cap 32768 truncations; scope-classifier block avg 640 est tok), surfaces 2+3 clean. **Surface 1 REGRESSION:** its regex `Pattern.compile("...\"((?:[^\"\\\\]|\\\\.)*)\"")` over the full mergedContent body **StackOverflowError'd 12× in 54 cycles (~22%)** — Java `java.util.regex` matches `*` by STACK RECURSION (one frame/char), so any body >~2KB overflows. Deterministic repro: OK@1000 chars, SOE@5000. Each SOE was caught by the scheduler's `LoggingErrorHandler` → **aborted the interpretation post-hook** (the exact capability surface 1 preserves). Unit + LLM-eval tests missed it — inputs too small ([[feedback-llm-probe-realistic-context]] generalizes: unit inputs must match real SIZE distribution, not just content).
- **FIX:** `InterpretationProtocolContract.compactArtifactContent` rewritten to an index-based, escape-aware scan (no recursive quantifier); Pattern/Matcher imports + MERGED_CONTENT_FIELD removed → `MERGED_CONTENT_KEY` string constant. Byte-identical output on all prior cases. +1 regression test (`largeMergedBody_isElidedWithoutStackOverflow`, ~60KB body). **Suite 2123/0.**
- **Run 2 field-confirm** (`/tmp/preflight_runs/2026-07-24_20-49-36`): **GOAL_FULFILLED, smoke 19/23** (both final+preserved, preserved_source=loop-preserved → T4-CA fulfilled hook fired), 67 cycles, 19 merges, peaked at final cycle. **0 StackOverflowError · 0 aborted cycles · 188 elision markers firing · 0 context-cap truncations.** T4-AP + all 3 T2-EP surfaces now field-verified clean.
- Smoke boot-failure in run 1 = target-project `LoanService` missing-bean DI defect (Loans-under-covered pattern), NOT framework; correctly boot-FAILED not false-completed.
- **COMMITTED `2f681f8`** on main (user OK'd), not pushed — `fix(interpretation): T2-EP surface 1 — index-based scan replaces the recursive regex` (2 files, +61/-20).

Run 2 live at handoff (driver pid was `957722`, JVM pid `974145`). **NEXT SESSION: read `scratchpad/smoke_stability-*.ledger` + the two remaining `/tmp/preflight_runs/2026-07-18_09-*/10-*` corpora for the finished series-3 scores.**
- **6 capped fresh runs: best = 15/23. Every FULFILLED run was a false completion (14, 15).** Recent baseline was 18–19 fresh / 23/23 once (2026-07-07). This is BELOW anything recent.

**Root-cause traced end-to-end (I rebooted run3's artifact independently → same 14/23, all 9 misses = the status-code family):**
1. **Model's status-code mapping decays across the 3 resources.** Books CORRECT (201/400/404 — all 7 pass). Members almost (only create→200-not-201 fails). Loans BADLY wrong — collapses every business-rule violation to generic `400`: not-found→should-be-404, book-on-loan→409, over-limit→422, return-again→409, all return 400.
2. **The spec states those codes VERBATIM** (`firestarter/bootstrap/seed.txt`: "404 if either not found; 409 if book is already on loan; 422 if exceeded"). So 7 of 9 misses = model ignoring EXPLICIT requirements; only create→201 is an unstated REST convention (fair guess).
3. **The verifier PASSED it because criteria coverage is ASYMMETRIC.** GoalStack of the fulfilled run: ~38 status/rule criteria for Books, ~12 Members, ~21 Loans (crude substring counts, ratio is the point). Loop verified the well-covered Books criteria, hit GOAL_FULFILLED; the thinly-covered members/loans status codes were never checked against the spec's stated codes → 400-collapse invisible to closure.

**Attribution (what this is NOT):** NOT wave 1 — `git diff HEAD~6 HEAD` shows the ONLY production change is `20e91e3` T4-CL touching `src/test/resources/application-test.yml` (graph isolation); nothing on the evolution/evaluation/controller/status-mapping path. NOT a model swap — same `qwen3-coder:30b` (digest unchanged since 2026-03-25, 262K ctx = no truncation), **temperature 0.7** (explains 14–15 vs 18–19 as sampling noise around the SAME weak capability; do NOT over-claim a hard regression on n=6). NOT scaffold/spec/harness drift — `seed.txt`, `smoke23.sh` unchanged.

**PROPOSED TICKET (→ FILED 2026-07-18 midday as T4-CO, see the block above):** ground spec-stated HTTP status codes as a criterion PER ENDPOINT, uniformly — the planner must author "POST /loans returns 409 when book on loan" etc. for every endpoint whose code the spec names, not richly-for-Books-thinly-for-Loans. This closes the false completion at the verifier BY CONSTRUCTION, independent of how the model draws. Tier: this is a designed-but-uneven policy (coverage depends on planner attention) → leans **T2**; if framed as "criteria grounding lacks a spec→per-endpoint-status binding capability" → **T4**. Related: [[project-false-completion-2]] (status-code family is the recurring precision residual), [[project-first-perfect-smoke-score]] (23/23 needed the full completion stack + luck), [[feedback-replay-over-batches]].

**METHODOLOGY CORRECTION (user pushback, keep):** I cited `run_profile.py` "shape-health 0/11 outside envelope" as if it reassured about the result — user: *"what do the shape healths mean? Earlier we got 23/23, most is not saying anything like that."* CORRECT. The 11 features (`merge_rate/reject_rate/inject_rate/build_fix_rate/skip_rate/proposal_rate/planner_only_share/max_inject_refires/inject_refire_share/merge_drought_share/tail_share`) are ALL internal loop DYNAMICS — none reads/compiles/boots the artifact. "0/11" means only "the loop churned at normal rates"; a 23/23 and a 14/23 run have IDENTICAL shape-health. **Never cite shape-health as evidence about output correctness — only the smoke score measures that.** T4-CI built it to catch pathological mechanics (starvation), not quality.

The jar scan can't see the JDK. Fix: `JdkPlatformTypeIndex` — live `ModuleFinder.ofSystem()` index (4603 names, 134 ms, once per JVM), java.base as its own view; imports pick across classpath∪platform; unique java.base name with no classpath claim = deterministic `jdk-platform` import-only hit; java.base-vs-classpath conflicts (`Pattern`: jakarta vs java.util.regex) ABSTAIN to registry/LLM (field runs show the registry answering those correctly).

Fix: `BomManagedVersionsService` (parent chain + import-scoped BOMs walked offline through the local repo, repo root derived from the project's own classpath, property interpolation, nearest-wins) consumed at `maybeAddDependency` — the ONE pom-write home omits the version element whenever the project's chain manages the artifact, for discovery/registry/LLM alike.

Jar-level role derivation stays sighting-gated.

## 2026-07-11 (Opus 4.8, evening) — the "run-quality regression since T4-BZ" is RETRACTED: it's NON-DETERMINISM (variance), not a bisectable commit; + the churn-to-deadline anatomy → one meaningful next step
- **⚠ 2026-07-13 CORRECTION to this block:** the "keeps resetting the counter" mechanism claim below was refuted by corpus replay — all four churn runs show zero injection-resets; see the block above.
- **Context:** picked up mid-bisect after a `/clear`. The 07-11 morning blocks below chased a "smoke dropped since T4-BZ" regression: `f698e64` called it a model trough, `3781975` RETRACTED that (e0b5bff=23/23 same env, n=1), then a user-directed single-run bisect blamed `7a687f5` (analysis SIGNALS NAME=strength → over-throttled injection) and reverted it (`9ce3b2d`). The prior session (Fable 5) then found `7a687f5` was a RED HERRING (throttle fires 2-3× in both good and bad runs) and, at its session limit, concluded the real symptom is planner starvation AND that single-run bisecting is unreliable because the framework is non-deterministic even at temp-0 (unseeded exploration jitter + HashMap render order + GPU FP). User's last instruction there: "It could be a combination of all changes. Yes, run the multi-run probability characterization." Full transcript: `~/Documents/autobuilder/T4-BZ_20260710`.
- **DID the multi-run characterization (this session).** Harness (`~/Documents/autobuilder/t4bz-regression-bisect/`): `run_characterization.sh` round-robins commits, **one fresh `git worktree add --detach` per run** (pristine firestarter by construction — NO `git clean`/`checkout -f`/`rm`, per user objection to blanket destruction), preflight-capped 45m each, `extract_metrics.py` (validated: reproduces planner/analysis/REPLAN discriminators), results → `results.tsv`. 7 runs before the user stopped it (enough).
- **RESULT — variance, not a commit.** e0b5bff (the "stable anchor" the whole bisect rested on) = **1 GOOD / 2 BAD** (23/23; deadline+build-failed; deadline 15/23). **87dfaed — the commit the single-run bisect fingered — scored 23/23.** HEAD 9ce3b2d = 18/23 fulfilled + a deadline. Every commit spans build-failed→23/23; distributions overlap almost completely. The morning's per-commit verdicts were single draws from one noisy distribution. **This RETRACTS "the regression is in my 51 commits"** — there is no bisectable code regression; low smoke is run-to-run variance (plus an evening environment skew: 4/7 runs hit the wall clock tonight vs mostly-fulfilling this morning). `9ce3b2d` names-only revert = confirmed neutral (a non-fix). Weak residual: HEAD was the only commit without a 23/23 (0/2) — inside the noise at n=2, not worth chasing.
- **THE MEANINGFUL FINDING (user steer: "not perfection, something meaningful, leading to better"). Churn-to-deadline anatomy, confirmed across all 4 bad runs:** productive early → hits ONE unfixable recurring defect (e.g. cannot-find-symbol; mutation `NOT_EVALUATED` because it won't compile) → grinds **8–32 byte-identical dead cycles** (up to ~40% of the run) to the 45-min cap, and the thrash sometimes DAMAGES the artifact (e0b5bff-130 ended `Unable to find main class` — the entry point got broken during the dead tail). **The loop KNOWS it's stuck** — every dead cycle prints `RECURRING_DEFECT_DOMINANT=3.00`, `repetition=true`, `COMPILE_DIAGNOSTIC_FED_BACK=3.00` — **but the halt decision never consults it.** `RootConvergenceTracker` (core/lifecycle) halts only on a coarse proxy: `cyclesSinceProgress >= cycleLimit(20)` where "progress" = a new completed-with-merge subgoal OR a new closure-edge injection — and the failing gate **re-injecting work for the same unfixable defect keeps resetting the counter** (`counting as root progress, not stall`, lines 79-85 + 121-126). Classic activity≠progress ([[feedback-activity-not-progress]]); the north-star gap ([[project-introspection-as-north-star]]): a self-model exists but no controller consults it. **Proposed one step (NOT built):** wire the stuck-signal into the continue/halt decision — when `RECURRING_DEFECT_DOMINANT`+`repetition`+no-accepted-merge-for-K all hold, halt or change strategy instead of grinding; bonus, stopping the dead tail preserves the last working artifact. Won't raise the good-run ceiling; makes the FAILURE mode honest/cheap instead of wasteful/self-damaging.
- **Open housekeeping:** ephemeral worktrees `/tmp/autobuilder-run-*` (~7GB, 8 of them) left for cleanup (`git worktree remove`); decide whether to revert the neutral `9ce3b2d`. Process note for self: this session over-invested in machinery to confirm a negative and pushed decisions onto a tired user — when a prior session already suspects "it's noise," a small confirmation + pivot beats a full characterization.
- **Next / the direction:** [[project-reliability-plan]] (2026-07-12; NOT filed tickets) — floor up, ceiling free. One grounded next move: stand up the reliability batch as the yardstick, then teach the loop to consult its stuck-signal at the halt decision — measure the floor before/after. Everything downstream stays a candidate the batch must justify. Every change measured as a batch distribution, never a single run.

The old stored form `withSignal(ROLE_PLANNER, 0.0)` had PRESERVED an existing planner signal via `reinforce(0.0)` (boost kept, age reset); the hard revoke starved the planner-replan escape hatch. On a subgoal evolution couldn't compile (861 cannot-find-symbol), analysis-unlock fired 84× deleting the planner window, so a later REPLAN_REQUESTED at urgency 3.0 could never reach an eligible planner (window 0.0) — planner frozen at cycle 8, kept off a hard halt only by the T2-EH brake. This was the exact documented "behavior-riskiest A14 change, fallback = grant-0 if flips show planner starvation." Applied the fallback (`b083b03`): grant-0, which `ParticipationWindow.grant` merges exactly like `reinforce(0.0)`. New `DeadlockGuardInvariantTest` case pins it. **Diagnostic method that worked: read the ranking breakdown — planner `urgency: 3.0, window: 0.0` was the smoking gun (wants to replan, can't).**

Clean boot (34.7s, no wiring errors). Smoke **14/23** = ONE cascading loan-ID `Integer cannot be cast to Long` bug in the LLM-generated Loan code (fails all 7 loan checks + 2 return checks; Books/Members 12/12 pass) — the variance axis / pre-existing false-completion family ([[project-false-completion-2]]), NOT a signal-system regression. Per the design note the T4-BZ claim was always truthfulness + deletion of a pathology class + a consultable self-model, explicitly NOT smoke-score improvement.
- **T4-BZ UMBRELLA COMPLETE (0→5).** Signals are now a DERIVED READOUT over mechanism carriers — 5 participation windows, a 12-kind request channel, 10 derived facts, 3 toggles — computed per read, never stored. The three-concerns-in-one-abstraction stored-Signal layer (strength cap-pinning, decay, reinforce-min ratchet, mutate) the T4-J audit measured mostly-inert is DELETED. A computed readout cannot go stale. [[project-t4bz-design-note]] has the full slice history + deviations.

The T4-BO lifetime instrumentation renamed off mortality wording (`Death`→`LifecycleEnd`, `deathCycle`/`deathReason`→`endCycle`/`endReason` incl. event payload; report script reads both key sets so old corpora parse). ## 2026-07-09 (Fable 5, evening) — T4-J Q1 DELETIONS SHIPPED + PHASE 3 PERTURBATION LAB BUILT AND SWEPT
- **Q1 deletions (`d0bb748`):** `AGENT_EXHAUSTED`, `EXPLORATION_REQUESTED`, `BACKGROUND_TICK` removed from BasicSignals + graph.html (zero emitters/readers per the Phase-1 map; BACKGROUND_TICK was noise in all five fitness sets). Suite 1883/0 at that point.

Sweep headlines: (1) planner/analysis fitness often EXACTLY equal — role saturation at 3.0 + fully-shared common set members; Phase-0's sub-jitter coin-flips are exact ties, no constant fixes them (missing tie-break/differentiator is the Q3/Q6 shape); an ULP-noise instrument bug was caught by hand-checking one implausible flip → epsilon-tier canonicalization; (2) PLANNING_THROTTLED blocks planner eligibility while BOOSTING planner fitness (PLANNER_SIGNALS membership questionable); (3) decayPerCycle flips NO same-cycle decision — Q7 decay calibration requires Phase 3d multi-cycle replay; strength/age are the same-cycle levers; (4) ROLE_MERGE top-rank boundary ≈2.0 — the score-plateau boost (2.0) is exactly calibrated to put merge on top (per-site split looks intentional, confirming Phase-1); (5) eligibility, not rank, often picks the executor (evolution ranks last yet executes via walk-down); (6) COMPILE_DIAGNOSTIC_FED_BACK 0 flips (Q1 confirmed behaviorally), EVOLUTION_FINGERPRINT_CHANGED 0 flips in the selection layer (its consumers live elsewhere — correct).
- **Phase 3c SHIPPED same evening (`4d434ba`):** `AgentPromptRenderer` renders the 5 prompts per production adapter derivations + line-diffs; per-signal x per-prompt reach table in the report. Headlines: interpretation prompt reached by 100% of perturbations (embeds full map incl. age/decay — decay's ONLY same-cycle consumer is that prompt text); analysis prompt = presence/strength only; dedicated paths confirmed (REPLAN_REQUESTED→planner; DIVERGENCE_REQUEST/REPETITION_DETECTED/SCORE_PLATEAU/STAGNATION→evolution); **evaluation prompt is signal-blind** (Q6 question). Also: user flagged "Lab"/"blanket-render" naming as vague → renamed to FrozenCycleObserver/FrozenStateFixtures/StubbedServices/AgentPromptRenderer/SignalPerturbationSweepTest, resources `/frozen-states/`; freeze pin + field-validation pin added earlier same evening (`fd792f3`, `4e849ea`, `ba2a674`). Full 3c detail in [[project-t4j-perturbation-lab]].
- **Phase 3d SHIPPED same night (`d19b153`, suite 1911/0):** `SignalTrajectoryReplay` — N-cycle replays, real selection machinery + scripted role effects. FIRST DECAY RESULT: MISSING_SYMBOL decayPerCycle sits on a wide decision-inert plateau (0.05/0.10/0.25 shift death cycle 20/11/5, flip ZERO decisions over 15-20 cycles in 3 scenarios; only 0.5 — faster than the role-participation window — diverges). Mechanism: equal planner+analysis boost can't reorder the pair; role-signal attenuation/decay lifecycle bounds their participation windows identically across variants. Calibration candidate #1 DEPRIORITIZED within [0.05,0.25]. Also: static variant stub livelocks the replay (withVariantsFollowingAcceptance() fixes); role signals die by attenuation while their agent still executes every cycle. Detail in [[project-t4j-perturbation-lab]].
- **Decay grids broadened to EVERY signal (user steer) — shortlist INVERTED:** ROLE_ANALYSIS decay diverges decisions at every grid point in both replay scenarios (it sets analysis's participation window; nothing re-emits it); ROLE_PLANNER sensitive where planner is eligible; census "decay-driven" diagnostics are plateau-inert. Mechanic found: Signal.reinforce takes min() of decays — decay ratchets downward under re-emission, so re-emitted signals (ROLE_EVALUATION/ROLE_EVOLUTION/REPLAN_REQUESTED...) are immune to state-side decay perturbation; emission-site variants are the follow-up.
- **Next (T4-J):** script-emission decay variants (the re-emitted family); Q4 pairwise ablations; then Phase 4 LLM probes (targets = the 5 dedicated-path signals + the 2 full-set renders) / Phase 2 read path before live layers 5-6. Open user-decision item: STEERING_SUPPRESS/RESTAGE_SUPPRESS = document-as-operator-switch vs delete; RECOMBINATION_REQUEST disposition.

- **Context:** user-driven design session reshaped T4-J from "tune 2 decay constants" into an open signal-system audit ([[project-t4j-design-note]], committed `adc9895`): which signals should decay vs not, which are obsolete vs missing, calibration LAST (Q1-Q7). Calibration shortlist shrinks to `MISSING_SYMBOL`, `PLANNING_THROTTLED`, `ROLE_MERGE`.** Also: 3 fully dead signals (`AGENT_EXHAUSTED`, `EXPLORATION_REQUESTED`, `BACKGROUND_TICK` — the last a member of ALL FIVE role fitness sets while never existing); 3 consumers-without-emitters (`RECOMBINATION_REQUEST` legacy; `STEERING_SUPPRESS`/`RESTAGE_SUPPRESS` = plausible intentional operator switches, only settable via `PUT /autobuilder/signals`); 3 more emit-only markers whose real decision carrier is a context field (`BUILD_FIX_LOCUS_STUCK`→`sameFailureStreak`, `IRREDUCIBLE_GAP`→rehab plumbing, `RECURRING_DEFECT_DOMINANT`→injected subgoal) — the first is also a live Q6-case-iii sighting (steering that bypasses the signal system). `EVOLUTION_FINGERPRINT_CHANGED` vs `EVOLUTION_OCCURRED` (1.00 co-occurrence) resolved: distinct consumers, NOT redundant.
- **Also this stretch:** MEMORY.md compacted 20.1KB→11KB (hook-triggered; one line per entry, duplicate binding-constraint entry merged).
- **Next:** Phase 3 (frozen-state perturbation lab) needs no further prerequisites; Phase 2 (TuningParameterStore) only before live/field phases. The Q1 deletions (3 dead signals) are safe pure wins whenever picked up — dispositions recorded in the map, not yet executed (per ticket-DoD discipline, they belong to the audit's execution, with user sign-off on the operator-switch question).

## 2026-07-09 (Sonnet 5) — RETRACTION: "evolution runs ~93% less under meta-ON" was an instrumentation artifact, not a real effect
- **Trigger:** user picked the thread back up after a `/clear`, pointed at the previous session's full transcript export (`/home/anugeshtu/Documents/autobuilder/analysis_meta-lane-deletion_run-statistics_20260709`) and re-asked the question that session was mid-investigating when it hit its context limit: *"if evolution really runs 93% less under meta-ON, how can score and duration still tie?"* That prior session had just found the paradox sharpen (ON shows MORE `mutation_merged` than `agent_selected(evolution)` — "up to 6.75 merges per evolution pick," logically impossible under a naive selection→mutation→merge chain) and was about to open a run's log to explain it when it ran out of context.
- **Root cause, found by reading `AdaptiveExecutionEngine` source directly:** `behaviourLogger.agentSelected(...)` is called from exactly TWO sites (~line 203, ~244), both inside the signal-driven fitness-ranked pass. `executeMetaSteps` (~line 509, the meta-plan dispatch path) calls `agent.execute(...)` directly and **emits no equivalent event at all** — confirmed by grepping every `agentSelected(` call site in `src/main`. Under meta-ON, most cycles' work is dispatched via the meta-plan path (the deliberation agent's own `deliberation_outcome.agentSteps` lists EVOLUTION 593 times across the 516 ON-arm cycles in the 8-pair aggregate, with only 2 pre-filtered) — so `agent_selected` was only ever counting the minority of ON-arm evolution executions that fell through to the fitness-pass second pass. The "93% less" number measured event coverage, not agent activity.
- **Corrected measurement** using `mutation_proposed` — emitted unconditionally by `EvolutionAgent` itself (`EvolutionAgent.java:231`, confirmed the only emission site) regardless of which dispatch path invoked it — on the same `/tmp/t4bo_aggregate/{on,off}_arm_all.ndjson` 8-pair data: **ON = 445 proposals / 516 cycles = 86.2/100cyc; OFF = 420 proposals / 664 cycles = 63.3/100cyc — evolution runs slightly MORE per cycle under meta-ON, not 93% less.** `mutation_merged` shows the same direction (ON 29.7/100cyc vs OFF 24.9/100cyc). This resolves the exact paradox that triggered the re-check.
- **What's retracted vs what stands:** RETRACTED — the per-role starvation table and the causal chain built on it (evolution-starvation → `REPETITION_DETECTED`/`SUBGOAL_EXHAUSTED` elevation via `PatternSignalAnalyzer.applyFingerprintRepetition` checking evolution's fingerprint history). That chain's premise (evolution runs 15x less) is false, so it needs independent re-diagnosis if those signal-rate deltas (which came from separate, valid T4-BO signal-lifetime data) are still to be explained. **UNAFFECTED** — the score tie (OFF mean 17.29 vs ON mean 17.43, based on smoke scores, never touched `agent_selected`) and the **T4-AI-2+5 deletion re-entry condition, which remains MET** on that basis alone. The aggregate `agent_selected`-per-cycle density difference (OFF 2.81 vs ON 1.30) itself is real and has an innocent explanation already on record: the meta prefix consumes a role's one-shot-per-cycle slot before the fitness pass runs, so fewer *fitness-pass* selections remain per cycle — it just doesn't mean fewer executions.
- **Filed T4-BY** (roadmap.md, Tier 4): no execution-count event exists for the meta-dispatch path at all; fix is to emit an equivalent event from `executeMetaSteps` at its `agent.execute(...)` call site. Scoped to evaporate if T4-AI-2+5's full deletion lands first (the path it instruments goes away).
- **Process note:** this is the same shape as the T1-FH retraction (2026-07-07) — a plausible, causally-grounded-sounding mechanism (complete with a specific source-code citation) still needs the instrumentation itself checked before being reported as measured. The previous session's own mid-investigation self-correction (a flawed sanity-check grep that briefly *contradicted* the 93% number before being fixed) reinforced false confidence in the wrong direction — the grep bug was real, but fixing it didn't validate the deeper premise (that `agent_selected` is a complete execution count), which nobody questioned until the user's "how can this be" pushback forced it.
- **How the flawed 93% number was originally computed (for the record — same bug, reproduced independently):** filter `behaviour.ndjson` events to `event=="agent_selected"`, group by `payload.role`, divide by total cycle count, ×100, per arm. That's a reasonable-looking query — the bug is invisible at the query level, only visible by reading `AdaptiveExecutionEngine` source to learn `agent_selected` has an unlogged second dispatch path. Independently re-running that exact query this session landed within ~2% of the original numbers (evolution: mine 86.1/5.6 per 100cyc vs original 87.86/5.85 — the residual gap is corpus-selection/denominator noise, not a different bug), which cross-validates that both sessions were computing the same (flawed) thing the same way.

## 2026-07-09 (Sonnet 5, cont.) — RE-DIAGNOSIS: the 6/8 elevated thrash signals track evolution's HIGHER execution rate under meta-ON, not starvation
- **Method:** computed per-100-cycle rates for all `signal_lifetime_observed` types on the same `/tmp/t4bo_aggregate/{on,off}_arm_all.ndjson` (8-pair, 516 ON-cycles / 664 OFF-cycles), then compared each signal's ON/OFF ratio against evolution's own corrected execution-rate ratio (`mutation_proposed`: 86.24/63.25 = **1.363**).
- **Rising cluster tracks evolution's ratio tightly (1.17×–1.40×, evolution itself is 1.36×):** REPETITION_DETECTED 1.40×, SUBGOAL_EXHAUSTED 1.31×, MISSING_SYMBOL 1.28×, DIVERGENCE_REQUEST 1.23×, SUBGOAL_SCOPE_MISMATCH 1.22×, SCOPE_OUT_OF_SCOPE 1.17×. **Mechanistically confirmed, not just correlated:** read `EvolutionAgent.updateFingerprint` (~line 272) — it runs on EVERY evolution execution and directly sets `REPETITION_DETECTED`/`DIVERGENCE_REQUEST` itself (source `"EvolutionExecutionAgent"`) when the new fingerprint repeats a recent one, and clears `REPETITION_DETECTED` when it doesn't. `MISSING_SYMBOL` (`AnalysisAgent`/`PlannerAgent` grounding checks) fires against whatever code evolution most recently wrote. `GoalExhaustionDetector`'s Rule A OR-consumes `STAGNATION`/`REPETITION_DETECTED` (previously confirmed in source). **Conclusion: these signals aren't elevated because evolution runs less — they're elevated because evolution runs slightly MORE (a higher fraction of cycles, ~86% vs ~63%), and several of them are LITERALLY WRITTEN by evolution's own per-execution bookkeeping. More executions mechanically produce more set/clear episodes.** No starvation needed as an explanation; the retracted causal chain had the sign backwards on top of the wrong premise.
- **Falling cluster (0.76×–0.92×, evolution-adjacent but on the "productivity" axis, not the "frequency" axis):** EVOLUTION_NOOP 0.76×, STAGNATION 0.82×, PLANNING_THROTTLED 0.83×, POTENTIALLY_COMPLETE 0.85×, LOW_CONFIDENCE 0.91×. `EVOLUTION_NOOP` (confirmed in source, `EvolutionAgent` ~line 172) fires when the LLM declares `#NoDiff` — the direct alternative outcome to producing `mutation_proposed`'s tracked diff. `STAGNATION`'s `pattern:empty-diff` source (`PatternSignalAnalyzer.applyNoMeaningfulChange`) is mechanistically the same family (evolution ran but produced an empty diff, just via the independent diff-checker rather than the LLM's own claim) — both are two different detectors of "unproductive evolution attempt." **Given mutation_proposed (genuine diffs) rose 1.36× while these unproductive-outcome signals fell to 0.76–0.82×, evolution attempts under meta-ON are both MORE FREQUENT and MORE LIKELY TO PRODUCE A REAL DIFF.** Plausible mechanism, architecturally grounded but not isolated with a direct A/B on prompt content: meta's per-step `request` text is rendered into the evolution prompt as a concrete, specific target (confirmed sample: `"Implement the GET /books endpoint with proper HTTP method and path mapping."`) vs. the fitness-driven path's more generic subgoal-level prompt — a sharper target plausibly makes the LLM less likely to conclude "already done." **This is the one part of the re-diagnosis still a hypothesis, not a proven mechanism** — would need a direct comparison of ON vs OFF evolution prompts/outcomes to confirm, not just the outcome-rate correlation.
- **`PLANNING_THROTTLED` (0.83×) is its own case, NOT explained by the evolution-frequency mechanism:** confirmed in source (`PlannerAgent` ~line 259/267) it's set only by the planner's OWN execution (skip-ratio / backlog-cap checks) — so its lower rate under ON is consistent with planner genuinely executing less often, which — unlike evolution — **has no path-independent proxy event to verify** (no `behaviourLogger` calls anywhere in `PlannerAgent.java`, confirmed by grep). This is a real, still-open question, not resolved by this re-diagnosis — and it's exactly the gap **T4-BY** exists to close (an execution-count event on the meta-dispatch path would apply to every role, not just evolution).
- **`ROLE_MERGE` is the sharpest outlier (0.41×, far outside every other cluster) and is explained by a DIFFERENT, correctly-scoped version of the original mechanism:** `ROLE_MERGE` is a role-PRIORITY signal (reinforced/attenuated when merge executes, consumed when its priority is spent) — not an execution-count proxy. `mutation_merged` (the actual outcome) is HIGHER under ON (1.19×, from the earlier retraction numbers), so merge itself is not suppressed. What's suppressed is the SIGNAL-DRIVEN priority mechanism's role in getting merge to run: under meta-ON, merge is frequently dispatched directly via an explicit ordered step in the meta plan rather than needing its priority signal to accumulate and then be spent — so the traditional "signal rises → role executes → signal clears" episode count drops even though merge activity itself rises. **This is the one piece of the original diagnosis that WAS correctly identifying a real mechanism (meta bypasses signal-driven selection) — it just got misapplied to evolution's total activity level, which the meta path does NOT suppress, instead of to the signal-priority bookkeeping specifically, which it does.**

## 2026-07-08/09 (Sonnet 5, cont.) — 8-PAIR META-LANE A/B COMPLETE (13 runs) + T4-BO AGGREGATE REPORT, user-requested extension
- **User asked for 4 more pairs after the 2-pair result reversed on pair 4's perfect-score run** (see prior block). Ran pairs 5-8 (runs F,G,H,I,J,K,L,M) alternating OFF/ON, always launching one at a time (shared ports/working dir), confirming meta-status via log grep before each wait.
- **FINAL SCORE TALLY (7 scoreable per arm — B/H(OFF) never produced a bootable artifact; I(ON) was manually ended mid-stall):** OFF = 17,18,14,16,13,21,22 (mean 17.29); ON = 16,16,15,23,22,11,19 (mean 17.43). **A genuine tie** — the mid-batch swings (pair 4/5 favoring ON hard incl. a 23/23 perfect score; pair 7 favoring OFF hard at 21 vs 11) washed out exactly as pure run-to-run variance predicts once enough pairs accumulated. This is now real evidence for "undetectable above variance," not a 2-pair hunch — **the T4-AI-2+5 code-deletion re-entry condition (several off-by-default runs, no regression) is MET.**
- **Separately discovered, then SHARPENED past the aggregate (user pushed twice — first on execution-time/signal-quality, then specifically on the thrash-signal percentages):** selections-per-cycle is a clean, zero-overlap mechanical split across ALL 13 runs — OFF clusters 2.54–3.07 (mean 2.81), ON clusters 1.04–1.44 (mean 1.30). Execution time and lifetime/correlation direction turned out to ALSO be near-ties (duration: OFF 49.1min vs ON 51.2min mean; 14/14 signals classify identically decayed-vs-cleared in both arms; 6/6 checked correlation pairs agree in direction). **But per-ROLE selection rates (not just the aggregate) reveal the real mechanism: evolution — the only mutation-producing role — runs ~93% LESS often per cycle under meta-ON (87.86→5.85/100cyc); evaluation −60%, analysis −31%, planner −39%; only merge rises (+30%).** This precisely explains why 6/8 stagnation/exhaustion/repetition-family signals fire 20–43% MORE often per 100 cycles under ON (normalized, not raw counts — raw counts were misleading since the arms have different total cycles): `REPETITION_DETECTED`'s emission (confirmed in source, `PatternSignalAnalyzer.applyFingerprintRepetition`) specifically checks the last 2+ EVOLUTION fingerprints for a repeat; `GoalExhaustionDetector`'s Rule A directly OR-consumes `STAGNATION`/`REPETITION_DETECTED` (confirmed in source), so `SUBGOAL_EXHAUSTED`'s rise is partly downstream of the same evolution-starvation, not independent. **The genuinely notable tension: despite evolution running ~15x less, final smoke scores STILL came out a statistical tie** — outcome quality is equivalent, but the PROCESS each lane uses to reach it is substantially different (meta measurably suppresses the core mutation-producing agent in favor of merge/reasoning roles). Worth carrying into T4-F (dynamic sub-agents) design as evidence the meta-agent's own LLM judgment meaningfully diverges from the deterministic fitness ranking's priorities — not just adds overhead, actively reallocates effort away from evolution.
- **T4-BO aggregate (7 OFF-arm runs, 651 combined cycles) is now T4-J-ready**, not just a 2-run preview: the "always cleared, decay constant is inert" family (`DIVERGENCE_REQUEST`, `STAGNATION`, `REPETITION_DETECTED`, `REPLAN_REQUESTED`, `EVOLUTION_NOOP`, `SUBGOAL_EXHAUSTED`, `SCOPE_OUT_OF_SCOPE`, `SUBGOAL_SCOPE_MISMATCH`, `POTENTIALLY_COMPLETE`) holds up cleanly at 3x+ the earlier sample size; `MISSING_SYMBOL`/`COMPILE_DIAGNOSTIC_FED_BACK` remain the only always-decayed (real tuning candidate) types; `PLANNING_THROTTLED`/`LOW_CONFIDENCE`/`ROLE_MERGE` turned out to be genuine mixes only visible at this scale. Correlation table now has real statistical weight (n in the hundreds per signal). Event-volume inflation re-checked on the full combined corpus: **12.3%**, confirming the earlier 21% (single short 13-cycle run) was exactly the predicted bootstrap-churn artifact, not a real problem.
- **User pushback, addressed directly, not deflected:** (1) *"why did earlier pairs go smoothly while later ones got long/troubled?"* — investigated concretely (ruled out state accumulation via `reset-on-start`, ruled out GPU contention via `nvidia-smi`, ruled out ollama mid-run instability — the reload observed was a normal post-kill idle-unload, not a crash); found ONE real confound (IntelliJ at 63% CPU from 21:12–22:51, overlapping run H + early I) but concluded the dominant explanation is ordinary documented run-variance (run B, the *second* run of the whole day, already hit a full deadline — "early=smooth" wasn't even fully true). (2) *"are you SURE nothing but the flag changed between E/F/G?"* — verified with git log (zero commits after 15:48:54, hours before run A even launched), `git status` (clean except the expected `firestarter/cache/` runtime artifact), and file mtimes on the key files (all predate run E). Confirmed: every one of the 13 runs was built from the identical committed tree; only `PREFLIGHT_EXTRA_FLAGS` varied.
- **Run I (meta-ON, pair 6) hit a genuine `T4-I: root convergence stall` → `SYSTEM_PAUSED` (decayPerCycle=0.0, permanent until cleared).** Looked like a hang (5+ min of silence) but was actually `AutobuilderService`'s one-shot log guard (`if (!systemPaused) { ...log...}`) — the scheduler kept ticking silently every cycle, correctly no-op'ing. Confirmed via thread dump (scheduler thread legitimately parked in the delay queue, not deadlocked). This is the T2-EH/T4-I/T4-AI-3 brake chain (shipped earlier this same session) working exactly as designed — recognizing leaf-repromotions without real root progress and halting cleanly instead of livelocking like the historic run-20 case. Manually terminated (`kill -TERM`) since the outcome was already decided (would just sit until the 85-min deadline).
- **Ops:** all 13 JVMs launched/watched/killed cleanly, ports verified free after each, corpora preserved under `/tmp/preflight_runs/2026-07-08_{15-52-05,16-35-22,18-02-33,18-48-11,19-05-13,20-17-26,20-28-30,21-20-20,22-49-14,23-49-51}` + `2026-07-09_{01-04-20,02-31-33,02-59-07}`; combined OFF-arm ndjson at `/tmp/t4bo_aggregate/off_arm_all.ndjson`; full block in `loop_report.md`.

## 2026-07-08 EVEN LATER (Sonnet 5, cont.) — T4-BO SHIPPED (`e8a3d8d`): signal-decay observability, validated on a real live run
- **Design note first** (`project_t4bo_design_note.md`), per user instruction ("think about the design/plan, then build it"). Core algorithmic decision: two per-cycle snapshots (signal map immediately before/after `tickSignals()`) are sufficient to attribute every signal death to "decayed" or "cleared" WITHOUT hooking any of the ~70 `withoutSignal(...)` call sites across the codebase (grep-confirmed) — verified against `Signal.tick()`'s actual semantics (age==0 → no decay on the first tick, since `reinforce` always resets age to 0) rather than re-deriving decay math.
- **Shipped:** `SignalLifetimeTracker` (new, `observability/`, pure state machine — zero Spring/logging deps, plain-map input/output, trivially unit-testable) + two new `BehaviourEvent`s (`SignalSnapshot`, `SignalLifetimeObserved`). Wired into all THREE `tickSignals` call sites in `CycleExecutor` (paused + T3-BL early-exit cycles decay signals too) via a new `tickSignalsTracked` helper, and into `AdaptiveExecutionEngine` right next to the EXISTING `agentFitnessRanked` call, reusing the already-defined `extractSignalSnapshot` helper — minimal-diff wiring, no new state anywhere else. Both new collaborators are optional `@Autowired(required=false)` fields (matching this exact class's existing `behaviourLogger`/`goalStateEventPublisher` pattern specifically so `CycleExecutorGoalLoopTest`'s manual positional-constructor call needed zero changes — `@RequiredArgsConstructor` would have broken it).
- **The causality-preserving snapshot point matters and is regression-tested:** `signalSnapshot` fires on the `snapshot` local ALREADY captured before any agent in the fitness pass executes — a new test proves an agent's own side-effect signal emission does NOT appear in that same cycle's snapshot (would otherwise let an action look like it "caused" a signal that in truth it created).
- **New offline analyser `scripts/signal_lifetime_report.py`** (single-pass, stdlib-only, no deps) — lifetime table (p50/p95, decayed/cleared breakdown, per signal type) + correlation table (`P(role selected | signal present) - P(role selected | signal absent)` per signal×role, joined on `cycleId`). Correctly handles the actual behaviour-log format (pretty-printed CONCATENATED JSON objects, not one-per-line despite the `.ndjson` extension — used `json.JSONDecoder.raw_decode` in a loop).
- **Validated on a REAL live run, not just synthetic fixtures** (per [[feedback-activity-not-progress]]): launched a short instrumentation-only `preflight.sh live 15` run, confirmed events flowing (`signal_snapshot`/`signal_lifetime_observed` counts climbing), then ran the analyser against the live `behaviour.ndjson` mid-run. **The correlations it produced were independently sensible** — matched the hand-coded `BasicSignals` groupings that were never fed into the analyser: `REPLAN_REQUESTED` correlated +0.86 with analysis and +0.46 with planner (it's in both `ANALYSIS_SIGNALS` and `PLANNER_SIGNALS`), `SCOPE_OUT_OF_SCOPE` correlated -0.90 with evolution (matches the scope guard's suppression intent). This is real evidence the whole pipeline measures something true, not just fires inertly.
- **Honest caveat recorded, not smoothed over:** event-volume inflation measured 21% on that short 13-cycle run (ticket's own target ≤20%), driven entirely by `signal_lifetime_observed` (~1.6 deaths/cycle — an early bootstrap-churn window with unusually high signal turnover); `signal_snapshot` itself tracks EXACTLY 1:1 with the pre-existing `agent_fitness_ranked` cadence, no surprise there. Flagged for re-check on a longer, steadier-state run rather than rounded up to "in budget."
- **`sessionEnd` handling:** deliberately NO shutdown hook — the analyser lists "still present at the final snapshot" informationally from the snapshot timeline instead of inventing a fake lifetime number (the snapshot-index space and the tracker's own cycle-counter space are genuinely different units; kept separate rather than silently conflated, a design refinement made DURING implementation and noted as such).
- **Ops:** the validation JVM died on its own partway through (no exception trace, clean abrupt stop — plausibly resource pressure after many back-to-back JVM launches today; unrelated to the T4-BO code, and it happened AFTER valid instrumentation data was already captured). `preflight.sh`'s own harness caught it correctly (`PREFLIGHT: FAIL mode=live failures: terminal=jvm-died`) and self-terminated cleanly — no orphaned process, no lingering port. 9 new tests (algorithm scenarios + 2 real end-to-end wiring tests driving actual `runCycle()` calls). Suite + preflight quick/full green BEFORE the validation run.
- **Next (not built here):** accumulate several more field runs (ideally >13 cycles) and re-run the analyser for a real T4-J-ready per-signal report; re-check the volume-inflation number on a longer run.

Traced it to an exact contradiction between two 2026-07-06 ships: T4-BN-3's scoped probe activates a STEER branch for a mixed (main+test) mutation whose test half breaks — `CompileProbeService.probe()` returns `COMPILE_ERROR` with files KEPT on disk (`rollbackPoint` set) so main work isn't sacrificed. But `EvaluationAgent` treated every `COMPILE_ERROR` as an already-rolled-back gate rejection: short-circuited to REJECTED without calling the LLM, never stored the steer rollback point, and its own LLM-rejection rollback branch was guarded on `COMPILE_OK` only. Net effect: `Merged: false` reported while the staged files (good main + broken test) silently persisted in `output/`, unrecorded. **Field-confirmed against the run-21 log** (`T4-BN-2: … keeping mutation, surfacing as steering` immediately followed by `T1-EC: … returning REJECTED`, 6 occurrences). Fix: gate short-circuit now requires `!filesOnDisk()`; a steered failure proceeds to normal LLM evaluation (ACCEPTED merges properly, REJECTED rolls back via a `filesOnDisk()`-keyed guard); T4-BX now relays rejection reasons for a steered-then-rejected mutation (semantic, not build-failure). 2 new regression tests.

Added `FactoryDuplicate` detection to `ManufacturedDependencyHeuristic`: flags a project-declared type manufactured with `new` in ≥2 different factory methods of one configuration class. Two corrections found reading the real run-23 artifact before writing code: the factory class was `@SpringBootApplication` (broadened `FACTORY_CLASS_MARKER`, which was `@Configuration`-only), and the manufactured store types carried NO component markers at all — gated on the factory-method idiom + project-declared types instead. **Verified the algorithm standalone (Python replay) before writing Java tests: flags ONLY run-23's two real defects (`BookRepository`, `MemberRepository`, correct method pairs) and is clean across all 5 preserved `/tmp` corpora + all 67 `backup/*/output` snapshots — zero false positives across the full 3-month corpus.** 6 new tests, all green first try.

## 2026-07-07 EVEN LATER — RUN 22 (90-min `preflight.sh live`, first with T5-BD graph capture): POSSIBLE FALSE COMPLETION, root cause CONFIRMED (T1-FJ)
- **RUN 22 (11:15→12:29, 90-min cap, 22 merges, corpus `/tmp/preflight_runs/2026-07-07_11-15-55`): terminal=GOAL_FULFILLED, smoke=10/23.** Per `AUTONOMOUS_TEST_LOOP.md`'s own table this exact combination is flagged "POSSIBLE FALSE COMPLETION... highest-value finding" — and this time the root cause is directly confirmed, not inferred.

- **Confirmed root cause:** generated `pom.xml`'s `maven-compiler-plugin` has no `<parameters>true</parameters>`; controllers use idiomatic bare `@PathVariable Long id` (no explicit `name=`) — valid Spring code, but without the compiler flag the binder can't resolve the parameter name at runtime, throwing `IllegalArgumentException` on most path/query-parameter endpoints (16 occurrences during smoke). **No test files were authored at all this run** (empty `src/test`), so there was nothing for the loop's own test gate to run even in principle — the artifact was genuinely never invoked by anything in the closure chain. Filed as **T1-FJ**.
- **⚠ Flagged, not built:** this is exactly the failure class the previously-stood-down **T4-BV (boot/execution gate)** targeted. Per [[feedback-ticket-definition-of-done]], T4-BV was stood down by the user and marked "do not rebuild without direction" — so T1-FJ is filed as a finding requiring the user's call on scope, not something to act on unilaterally.
- **T5-BD graph capture: first real-run validation, clean.** `dump_graph.sh` (shipped this session, `7bfb2a8`) captured 10,381 nodes / 5,659 relationships into the corpus alongside the usual log/GoalStack/artifact set — online, no Neo4j disruption.
- **Applying [[feedback-verify-at-decision-timestamp]] here:** the smoke test and pom.xml/controller reads were done directly against the PRESERVED KILLED artifact (`$corpus/artifact`), which is the actual completed state at GOAL_FULFILLED time (root completed at 12:28:15, JVM killed ~12:28:20, artifact copied immediately after) — no stale-comparison risk this time; the state examined IS the state that was judged fulfilled.

## 2026-07-07 LATE — RUN 20 = LIVELOCK / false non-halt (NEW failure class, distinct from false-completion & false-give-up)
- **RUN 20 (00:43→killed 02:05, PRE-grounding-gate jar, proven flag family: depth-first + cap 6 + subgoal-retry + divide-on-exhaustion + structure-query): 343+ cycles, ~80 min, but LIVELOCKED since 00:59:38 — 283 identical cycles over 63 min, last real merge 01:46, zero progress after.** Not terminal (no FULFILLED/EXHAUSTED/PAUSED, log never silent) → **the preflight `live` watcher would NOT have caught it** (it only breaks on those markers + silent-900s; an actively-logging non-progressing spin is a blind spot — candidate watcher fix: `completed-with-merge N -> N` unchanged across M cycles).
- **The deadlock — three mechanisms in mutual contradiction, no exit:** every cycle runs the identical 4 steps: (1) `T3-BL: all criteria banked → Applying FULFILLED early exit`; (2) `T2-EI D2: refusing FULFILLED for endpoint work item — POST /loans still has no request mapping in the output`; (3) `T2-EH verify-before-pause promoted 1 of 1 active leaf`; (4) `T2-EH … broke the convergence stall (completed-with-merge 20 -> 20) — not halting`. Can't **close** (D2 refuses, correctly), can't **halt** (T2-EH keeps "breaking the stall" on a 20→20 no-op promotion), can't **fix** (`SUBGOAL_SCOPE_MISMATCH=1.00` suppresses evaluation/merge; mode=UNDIRECTED, only ROLE_ANALYSIS live; merge agent selected 18× after 01:00 but nothing lands).
- **The sharp part — two gates disagree on ONE binary ground-truth fact.** The stuck leaf `goal-0d2118ff` was itself spawned by the structure machinery to repair the gap: description *"Provide the POST /loans endpoint … no request mapping for it exists in the output"*, criterion *"A request mapping for POST /loans exists in the application sources."* **T3-BL banked that criterion as MET; D2 reads the same source and says ABSENT.** D2 is right — I read the live `LoanController.java`: it has `@GetMapping` (GET /loans) + `@PostMapping("/{id}/return")` but **no plain `@PostMapping` for POST /loans (create/lend)** — the core endpoint is genuinely missing. This rhymes ABSTRACTLY with the text-vs-structure disagreement seen elsewhere (a criteria/text read vs a structural read), but runs are variant BY DESIGN — this is a family resemblance, not a repeat of any prior run; here the text side read MET while structure read ABSENT (the direction differs run to run). Structure (D2) is right to refuse; T3-BL should not have banked it.
- **Levers (hypotheses):** (a) T3-BL criteria-banking must reconcile against D2's structural view before declaring "all banked" — structure wins; (b) T2-EH must not treat a promotion yielding `N -> N` (no merge delta) as "stall broken" — that's the non-halt engine; (c) when D2 refuses closure, the repair leaf's actuator must be exempt from `SUBGOAL_SCOPE_MISMATCH` suppression, else the only fixer is disarmed.
- **SHIPPED (lever b, the halt-fix) 2026-07-07:** `RootConvergenceTracker` — a verify-before-pause promotion now only breaks the stall if `rootCompleted || progressAfter > progressNow` (real completed-with-merge movement); a bare re-promotion that leaves the count flat is no longer counted as progress → the run PAUSES for review instead of spinning. Regression pinned in `RootConvergenceTrackerTest.verifyPromotesButRootMetricStaysFlat_haltsInsteadOfSpinning` (proven fail-before/pass-after via `preflight quick` with the fix stashed — 1/130 fails without it, green with). Run-20 GoalStack committed as fixture `livelock-nonhalt-run20.json` + shape-guard in the scenario test (repair leaf ACTIVE while a child claims the endpoint COMPLETED). Levers (a) and (c) FILED 2026-07-07 as **T2-EL** (reconcile T3-BL criteria-banking vs the D2 structural view — structure wins) + **T2-EM** (exempt a structural repair leaf's actuator from `SUBGOAL_SCOPE_MISMATCH` suppression); both T2 (wire existing capability), revisit after the field run.
- **Also 2026-07-07 (user steer):** renamed `KillerRunRegressionScenarioTest` → `PreservedRunRegressionScenarioTest` and purged all "killer" prose from src/scripts — "killer" added to [[feedback-neutral-vocabulary]] avoid-list (naming-bias reflex). New [[feedback-runs-are-variant-by-design]]: runs are variant BY DESIGN, keep cross-run resemblances abstract, never "same as run N".
- **Ops:** killed PID 1197155 (SIGTERM→SIGKILL; port 8081 freed). Corpus preserved `backup/20260707_run-20_livelock/` (autobuilder log + behaviour ndjson + console + GoalStack + output_snapshot, 19M). `preflight.sh quick` PASS after kill (tree green). This was a MANUAL launch (no 45-min cap) — hence 80 min; preflight live would have deadlined it at 45 but still mislabeled it "data."

Brake counts creations+reopens only. `injectedIds` carries reopened twins so T1-FF criteria attachment applies.

## Tools added this session
- **`fast` Spring profile** (`--spring.profiles.active=dev-auto,fast`): overhead-trim only (`inter-cycle-delay-ms: 1` — NOT 0, which crashes startup: invalid @Scheduled fixedDelay). ~10-15% faster; LLM-inference is the floor. For fast CORRECTNESS checks use the deterministic harness (single-call, seconds), not a loop.
- Run-health probe pattern: `/tmp/bisect_run.sh` (build + capped plain + OK-ratio/cfs/controllers). Run backups under `/tmp/*-ab/`, `/tmp/drive-iter*`, `/tmp/t4bn2-validation/`.

## CRITICAL infra gotcha (cost us memory this session)
**`.claude-memory/` IS the loading memory** — `~/.claude/projects/.../memory` resolves to the repo's `.claude-memory/`. So `git checkout`/branch/cherry-pick that changes `.claude-memory` **mutates the live memory that loads next session.** Branching from main (which lacks session memos) silently deleted `project_completion_binding_constraint.md` + `project_t4bq_naming_consistency_ab.md` from the loading memory until recovered via `git checkout checkpoint -- .claude-memory/`. **Before switching branches, ensure `.claude-memory` is committed on the target branch, or memory will appear to vanish.**
